\\\"sed -n '1,260p' memory.md\\\"\",\"aggregated_output\":\"# Memory\\n\\n## 项目定位\\nEC-HTE 研究目标是纠正 LLM 生成的分类 effect modifier 中的差异性误分类对 CATE/HTE 估计造成的结构性偏差。首要可证伪机制是：当 subgroup misclassification rate 与真实 subgroup CATE magnitude 正相关时，单一全局 confusion-matrix correction 会系统性 under-correct 高误差/高效应 subgroup，并 over-correct 低误差/低效应 subgroup，因此可能比 uncorrected 更差。\\n\\n## 执行约束\\n当前 `PROJECT_BRIEF.md` 位于 `/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research/projects/ec_hte_codex_a/docs/PROJECT_BRIEF.md`，不在本项目知识文件白名单目录。项目配置中的执行服务器 `seetacloud-38274` 当前不在 MCP 可用服务器列表中，GPU 实验启动前需要 Director 修正资源绑定或重新分配可用服务器。\\n\",\"exit_code\":0,\"status\":\"completed\"}}\n./logs/workers/worker_research_baseline_protocol_ec_hte_20260531T153440.log:8:{\"type\":\"item.completed\",\"item\":{\"id\":\"item_2\",\"type\":\"command_execution\",\"command\":\"/bin/bash -lc \\\"sed -n '1,240p' /home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research/projects/ec_hte_codex_a/docs/PROJECT_BRIEF.md\\\"\",\"aggregated_output\":\"# EC-HTE Codex Scheme A\\n\\n## EC-HTE: Error-Corrected Heterogeneous Treatment Effect Estimation Under LLM Annotation Misclassification\\n\\n## Problem and Motivation\\n\\nWhen NLP researchers use LLM-generated annotations as effect modifiers in causal heterogeneous treatment effect (HTE) analysis, a previously unrecognized structural bias arises: LLM misclassification rates covary with the true conditional average treatment effect (CATE). Subgroups where LLMs err most in annotation (e.g., marginalized populations, linguistically complex cases) are often the same subgroups where treatment effects are largest. This creates a confounding between measurement error and causal heterogeneity that cannot be resolved by any global correction method—including arm-specific rectifiers (Mozer, arXiv:2603.19160), which address average treatment effect (ATE) bias but are algebraically incapable of correcting subgroup-level CATE distortions.\\n\\nThis problem is urgent. LLM-as-annotator has become the default pipeline for computational social science (Audinet de Pieuchon et al., EMNLP 2025, benchmarking DSL vs. PPI debiasing; StratPPI, Fisch et al., NeurIPS 2024, improving PPI via stratification), and researchers increasingly use LLM annotations not just for ATE estimation (where DSL and PPI provide corrections) but for subgroup analysis and effect modification—yet no correction method exists for this latter use case. Messing (arXiv:2604.11581) documents that hidden measurement error in LLM pipelines causes naive confidence intervals to under-cover by 40–60%, but this analysis remains at the aggregate level. Ashwin et al. (Sociological Methods & Research, 2025) provide direct empirical evidence that LLM coding errors in refugee interview transcripts correlate systematically with respondents' refugee status, gender, and education level—precisely the variables used as effect modifiers in downstream causal analysis. Any study using LLM-annotated text variables as moderators produces unreliable heterogeneity findings whenever annotation error rates differ across subgroups—and Ashwin et al. show this is the norm, not the exception.\\n\\n**Key anti-intuitive finding: global correction can actively worsen CATE estimates.** Consider a two-subgroup example. Subgroup A has high misclassification (25%) and large CATE (0.5 SD); Subgroup B has low misclassification (5%) and small CATE (0.1 SD). A global correction uses a pooled confusion matrix (~15% error), which under-corrects Subgroup A and over-corrects Subgroup B. The net effect: globally-corrected CATE estimates can have *larger* bias than uncorrected estimates. This is not an edge case—it occurs whenever the misclassification pattern and CATE structure are positively correlated, which empirical evidence suggests is common in practice.\\n\\n## Core Contributions\\n\\nThis paper makes four contributions:\\n\\n1. **Problem identification and formalization.** We identify and formalize measurement-error-induced CATE distortion—a new class of bias arising when LLM annotation errors in effect modifiers are differential across subgroups and correlated with the true heterogeneity structure. We show this is structurally distinct from: ATE-level debiasing (Mozer, arXiv:2603.19160; Egami et al., NeurIPS 2023 DSL; Zrnic & Candès, ICML 2024), CATE-to-GATE aggregation bias (Persson et al., arXiv:2602.20383, where bias originates from aggregation, not measurement error), and conditional inference with outcome prediction noise (Sui et al., arXiv:2603.05575 PPCI, which assumes the conditioning variable is correct). We prove that global correction can *increase* CATE bias relative to uncorrected estimates when misclassification and CATE heterogeneity are positively correlated.\\n\\n2. **Actionable diagnostic criterion (Bias Bound).** We derive a finite-sample bound: the bias of globally-corrected CATE estimates is proportional to the product of (a) misclassification heterogeneity across subgroups and (b) CATE heterogeneity across subgroups. This product yields a practical diagnostic: given pilot data, any researcher can compute this quantity *before* running full CATE analysis to determine whether global correction suffices or stratified correction is necessary. This diagnostic has standalone value independent of the EC-HTE framework—it applies to any CATE analysis using LLM-annotated effect modifiers. The bound implies that bias is worst when annotation is least accurate for subgroups where treatment effects are largest—empirically common (Ashwin et al., 2025).\\n\\n3. **EC-HTE: A three-stage correction framework** providing subgroup-specific misclassification correction, valid confidence intervals, and optimal annotation allocation (detailed below).\\n\\n4. **Empirical validation** via semi-synthetic benchmarks with controlled DGPs (including systematic variation of misclassification-CATE correlation direction) and a real-data misclassification pattern validation (Ashwin et al., 2025, Rohingya interview corpus).\\n\\n## Why Not Just Use Expert Labels?\\n\\nA natural objection: why not skip LLM annotation entirely and estimate CATEs using only expert-labeled data? The answer is a bias-variance tradeoff specific to CATE estimation. With a budget of ~2,000 expert labels distributed across k subgroups, each subgroup contains ~2,000/k observations. Honest causal forests (Wager & Athey, JASA 2018) split this further into estimation and splitting samples, leaving each cell with ~1,000/k observations—insufficient for detecting moderate heterogeneity when k ≥ 4.\\n\\nEC-HTE resolves this by using the full unlabeled corpus (potentially hundreds of thousands of LLM-annotated observations) for low-variance CATE estimation, with expert labels only for calibrating subgroup-specific misclassification. This extends the PPI/DSL paradigm—combining noisy large-scale with clean small-scale data—from ATE to CATE, where data demands make the combination even more critical.\\n\\n## Method: Three-Stage Framework\\n\\n**Stage 1: Hierarchical Bayesian Subgroup-Specific Misclassification Estimation.** For each effect-modifier subgroup g ∈ {1, …, k}, we estimate a confusion matrix C_g between LLM annotation and expert gold standard using a hierarchical Bayesian model with partial pooling. The hierarchy allows low-sample subgroups to borrow strength from the global structure while permitting genuine cross-subgroup variation. This addresses a key tension: per-subgroup independent estimation (high variance when subgroup expert-label counts are small) vs. single global estimation (biased when misclassification rates genuinely differ). For computational scalability in large-scale simulation studies, we use variational inference (VI) as the primary fitting method, with MCMC validation on representative configurations in the appendix.\\n\\n**Stage 2: Corrected CATE Estimation with Sandwich Variance Adjustment.** Corrected effect-modifier assignments are fed into an honest causal forest (Wager & Athey, JASA 2018). Because Stage 1 introduces nuisance parameter uncertainty, naive confidence intervals will under-cover. We derive a sandwich variance inflator propagating Stage 1 uncertainty into Stage 2 CIs, ensuring valid coverage. Bootstrap-based CIs serve as a robustness check.\\n\\n**Stage 3: Adaptive Expert Annotation Allocation.** Given a fixed expert budget B, we optimize allocation across subgroups by jointly minimizing misclassification estimation uncertainty and CATE estimation variance. The allocation criterion targets subgroups where the product of misclassification uncertainty and CATE magnitude is largest—precisely where annotation errors cause the most CATE distortion (as predicted by the bias bound).\\n\\n**Cold-start protocol.** Since Stage 3 requires initial estimates of both misclassification rates and CATE magnitudes, we employ a multi-batch protocol: (a) allocate 20–30% of B as a uniform pilot batch; (b) estimate initial confusion matrices and CATE values; (c) allocate remaining budget adaptively. We include pilot ratio sensitivity analysis (10%, 20%, 30%) in the experiments.\\n\\n## Related Work and Differentiation\\n\\n**Thread 1: LLM annotation debiasing (ATE-level).** DSL (Egami et al., NeurIPS 2023), PPI and its algebraic equivalence to survey-sampling difference estimators (Mozer, arXiv:2603.19160), active inference (Zrnic & Candès, ICML 2024), stratified PPI (StratPPI, Fisch et al., NeurIPS 2024), active multi-predictor PPI (AM-PPI, arXiv:2605.08429), and benchmarking thereof (Audinet de Pieuchon et al., EMNLP 2025). All locked to population-level estimands. Messing (arXiv:2604.11581) diagnoses hidden measurement error causing 40–60% CI under-coverage but does not extend to causal HTE correction.\\n\\n**Thread 2: HTE/CATE estimation methods.** Honest causal forests (Wager & Athey, JASA 2018), DR-learner, CATE-to-GATE aggregation bias correction (Persson et al., arXiv:2602.20383, shrinkage for aggregation-induced bias assuming clean covariates), and assumption-lean HTE detection under mismeasured modifiers (Boileau et al., arXiv:2512.03254, hypothesis testing for whether heterogeneity exists, not CATE estimation—complementary to EC-HTE). CATE evaluation standards established in \\\"Really Doing Great at Estimating CATE?\\\" (NeurIPS D&B 2021).\\n\\n**Thread 3: Conditional inference with ML predictions.** PPCI (Sui et al., arXiv:2603.05575) extends PPI to conditional functionals at fixed test points via kernel localization. Critical distinction: PPCI addresses outcome prediction noise at a correctly-specified conditioning point; EC-HTE addresses the situation where the conditioning variable (effect modifier) itself is misclassified. PPCI's localization anchors on correct covariate values—when the anchor itself is wrong (categorical misclassification), kernel localization cannot help.\\n\\n**Thread 4: Measurement error correction for ML-generated variables.** ForestIV (Bu et al., INFORMS JDS 2023) uses random forest tree tuples as instrumental variables, but requires IV exclusion restrictions and is limited to regression settings, not CATE with categorical effect modifiers. Jerzak et al. (arXiv:2509.12416, SRI framework) provide valid inference under noisy annotation but do not address effect modifier misclassification's impact on HTE. SIMEX handles continuous measurement error models but does not directly apply to categorical misclassification.\\n\\n**Thread 5: Causal inference with textual variables.** Feldman et al. (arXiv:2602.15730) address latent textual treatment estimation using SAE + residualization, assuming clean effect modifiers. Nwankwo et al. (arXiv:2502.10605, NeurIPS 2025 Workshop) optimize annotation allocation for doubly robust ATE with missing outcomes—different estimand (ATE vs. CATE), different error source (outcome missingness vs. effect modifier misclassification), different optimization landscape.\\n\\nEC-HTE sits at the intersection of Threads 1–4: correcting LLM misclassification specifically in effect modifiers for valid CATE estimation.\\n\\n## Validation Design\\n\\n### Semi-Synthetic Experiments\\n\\n**Corpus.** Political science text corpus with real-world linguistic variation (e.g., immigration framing data or partisan tweet datasets with known ideological annotations).\\n\\n**DGP Matrix.** Core factors:\\n- Misclassification severity: high (25–30%) vs. low (5–10%)\\n- CATE heterogeneity magnitude: large (>0.3 SD) vs. small (<0.1 SD)\\n- Subgroup count: k=3 vs. k=6\\n- **Misclassification-CATE correlation direction**: positive (high-error subgroups have high CATE, worst-case) vs. negative (high-error subgroups have low CATE, best-case)\\n\\nSelective design: 12–14 configurations covering all worst-case/best-case combinations for the key factors, rather than full 2⁴=16 factorial.\\n\\nTreatment assignment T ~ Bernoulli(0.5); potential outcomes Y(1), Y(0) generated with known ground-truth CATE τ(x) varying by subgroup.\\n\\n**Replications.** ≥ 200 per configuration.\\n\\n**Sample sizes.** N ∈ {2000, 5000, 10000}.\\n\\n**Expert label budget.** B ∈ {100, 500, 1000, 2000} to produce budget vs. performance curves. All methods compared under identical total budget.\\n\\n**LLMs.** Three annotators: GPT-4.1 (proprietary frontier), Qwen 3.5 (open-source large), Llama 3.3 (open-source, different family). Fourth LLM as appendix robustness check.\\n\\n### Comparison Methods (all under fixed expert annotation budget B)\\n\\n1. **Uncorrected**: LLM annotations used directly as effect modifiers in causal forest.\\n2. **Globally-corrected**: Single confusion matrix → corrected annotations → causal forest.\\n3. **Arm-specific (Mozer-style)**: Separate confusion matrices for treatment/control arms → causal forest.\\n4. **SIMEX + causal forest**: SIMEX-based measurement error correction → causal forest.\\n5. **Calibration-only + causal forest**: Platt scaling / temperature scaling → calibrated probabilities as soft effect modifiers → causal forest.\\n6. **EC-HTE (full)**: Hierarchical Bayesian subgroup-specific correction → sandwich-adjusted causal forest → adaptive allocation.\\n\\n### Evaluation Metrics\\n\\n- **PEHE**: √E[(τ̂(x) − τ(x))²], overall and per-subgroup.\\n- **Subgroup CATE bias**: |E[τ̂_g] − τ_g| for each subgroup g.\\n- **CI empirical coverage**: Fraction of replications where true CATE falls within 95% CI (target: 0.95).\\n- **CI width**: Efficiency measure at fixed coverage.\\n- **GATE RMSE**: RMSE of group average treatment effect estimates.\\n- **Bound tightness**: Observed bias vs. theoretical bound prediction.\\n\\n### Ablation Studies\\n\\n**Stage 1 ablation (partial pooling):**\\n- (a) Hierarchical Bayesian with VI (partial pooling)\\n- (b) Per-subgroup independent confusion matrices (no pooling)\\n- (c) Single global confusion matrix (full pooling)\\n- (d) Prior sensitivity: 2–3 prior settings (weakly informative, moderately informative, diffuse) on 1–2 representative DGP configurations\\n\\n**Stage 2 ablation (variance adjustment):**\\n- (a) Sandwich variance-adjusted CIs\\n- (b) Bootstrap-based CIs\\n- (c) Naive CIs (no adjustment)\\n→ Compare empirical coverage rates.\\n\\n**Stage 3 ablation (allocation strategy):**\\n- (a) Joint criterion (misclassification uncertainty × CATE magnitude)\\n- (b) Misclassification-uncertainty-only allocation\\n- (c) CATE-magnitude-only allocation\\n- (d) Uniform allocation\\n- (e) Pilot ratio sensitivity: 10%, 20%, 30% of B as uniform pilot batch\\n→ Compare PEHE at fixed total budget B.\\n\\n**Diagnostic validation:** Systematically vary misclassification heterogeneity and CATE heterogeneity (including correlation direction); produce heatmap of EC-HTE improvement over global correction vs. (product of heterogeneity measures). Verify that the theoretical bound predicts when global correction fails and when it suffices. Specifically demonstrate that global correction is worse than uncorrected in positive-correlation configurations.\\n\\n**CATE estimator robustness (appendix):** In 1–2 worst-case configurations, replace causal forest with DR-learner to verify EC-HTE's correction generalizes beyond a single CATE estimator.\\n\\n### Real-Data Application (Ashwin et al., 2025)\\n\\n**Dataset.** Rohingya refugee interview transcripts (Ashwin et al., Sociological Methods & Research 2025). This dataset's primary role is validating the premise of EC-HTE—that LLM misclassification is differential—rather than evaluating CATE estimation accuracy (the dataset lacks a natural causal structure).\\n\\n**Validation goals:**\\n1. **Differential misclassification pattern confirmation**: Quantify and visualize how LLM error rates vary across subgroups defined by refugee status, gender, education.\\n2. **Leave-expert-out calibration test**: Treat full human labels as ground truth, subsample B expert labels, evaluate whether EC-HTE's hierarchical confusion matrix estimates recover the true subgroup-specific error patterns better than global estimation.\\n3. **Diagnostic criterion demonstration**: Compute the misclassification-heterogeneity × CATE-heterogeneity product on this dataset (constructing a plausible causal question from available variables) to demonstrate the diagnostic's operability.\\n4. **Budget curve**: Accuracy of confusion matrix recovery vs. expert label budget B.\\n\\nCATE estimation accuracy is rigorously evaluated in the semi-synthetic experiments where ground-truth CATE is known.\\n\\n## Scope and Limitations\\n\\nEC-HTE targets **categorical effect modifiers** (stance, topic, sentiment, entity type, etc.)—the dominant use case when LLM annotations serve as moderators in computational social science. When the effect modifier is a continuous variable that has been discretized (binned), misclassification includes both LLM errors and discretization error; the current framework does not disentangle these sources. Extension to continuous effect modifiers with measurement error (where SIMEX-type approaches may be more natural) is future work.\\n\\n## Broader Applicability\\n\\nWhile our validation focuses on CSS, the same problem structure arises whenever ML-predicted categorical variables serve as effect modifiers: in medical research (ML-classified tumor subtypes as moderators of drug efficacy), education (NLP-coded classroom behaviors moderating intervention effects), and policy evaluation (automated classification of administrative characteristics). EC-HTE's framework and diagnostic criterion apply directly to these settings.\\n\\n## Expected Contributions\\n\\nEC-HTE provides (1) the first formal identification of measurement-error-induced CATE distortion in LLM-annotated pipelines, including the proof that global correction can worsen estimates; (2) an actionable diagnostic (the misclassification-heterogeneity × CATE-heterogeneity product) with standalone value for any CATE analysis; (3) a practical three-stage correction framework with valid inference; and (4) comprehensive empirical validation. The framework serves any study using LLM-annotated categorical text variables as effect modifiers in causal analysis.\\n\\n\",\"exit_code\":0,\"status\":\"completed\"}}\n./logs/workers/worker_research_baseline_protocol_ec_hte_20260531T153440.log:20:{\"type\":\"item.completed\",\"item\":{\"id\":\"item_8\",\"type\":\"web_search\",\"id\":\"ws_0c5b75839698f20a016a1c574951148191b022f6f334a23a12\",\"query\":\"categorical -lc \\\"sed -n '1,220p' docs/PROJECT_BRIEF.md\\\"\",\"aggregated_output\":\"# EC-HTE Codex Scheme A\\n\\n## EC-HTE: Error-Corrected Heterogeneous Treatment Effect Estimation Under LLM Annotation Misclassification\\n\\n## Problem and Motivation\\n\\nWhen NLP researchers use LLM-generated annotations as effect modifiers in causal heterogeneous treatment effect (HTE) analysis, a previously unrecognized structural bias arises: LLM misclassification rates covary with the true conditional average treatment effect (CATE). Subgroups where LLMs err most in annotation (e.g., marginalized populations, linguistically complex cases) are often the same subgroups where treatment effects are largest. This creates a confounding between measurement error and causal heterogeneity that cannot be resolved by any global correction method—including arm-specific rectifiers (Mozer, arXiv:2603.19160), which address average treatment effect (ATE) bias but are algebraically incapable of correcting subgroup-level CATE distortions.\\n\\nThis problem is urgent. LLM-as-annotator has become the default pipeline for computational social science (Audinet de Pieuchon et al., EMNLP 2025, benchmarking DSL vs. PPI debiasing; StratPPI, Fisch et al., NeurIPS 2024, improving PPI via stratification), and researchers increasingly use LLM annotations not just for ATE estimation (where DSL and PPI provide corrections) but for subgroup analysis and effect modification—yet no correction method exists for this latter use case. Messing (arXiv:2604.11581) documents that hidden measurement error in LLM pipelines causes naive confidence intervals to under-cover by 40–60%, but this analysis remains at the aggregate level. Ashwin et al. (Sociological Methods & Research, 2025) provide direct empirical evidence that LLM coding errors in refugee interview transcripts correlate systematically with respondents' refugee status, gender, and education level—precisely the variables used as effect modifiers in downstream causal analysis. Any study using LLM-annotated text variables as moderators produces unreliable heterogeneity findings whenever annotation error rates differ across subgroups—and Ashwin et al. show this is the norm, not the exception.\\n\\n**Key anti-intuitive finding: global correction can actively worsen CATE estimates.** Consider a two-subgroup example. Subgroup A has high misclassification (25%) and large CATE (0.5 SD); Subgroup B has low misclassification (5%) and small CATE (0.1 SD). A global correction uses a pooled confusion matrix (~15% error), which under-corrects Subgroup A and over-corrects Subgroup B. The net effect: globally-corrected CATE estimates can have *larger* bias than uncorrected estimates. This is not an edge case—it occurs whenever the misclassification pattern and CATE structure are positively correlated, which empirical evidence suggests is common in practice.\\n\\n## Core Contributions\\n\\nThis paper makes four contributions:\\n\\n1. **Problem identification and formalization.** We identify and formalize measurement-error-induced CATE distortion—a new class of bias arising when LLM annotation errors in effect modifiers are differential across subgroups and correlated with the true heterogeneity structure. We show this is structurally distinct from: ATE-level debiasing (Mozer, arXiv:2603.19160; Egami et al., NeurIPS 2023 DSL; Zrnic & Candès, ICML 2024), CATE-to-GATE aggregation bias (Persson et al., arXiv:2602.20383, where bias originates from aggregation, not measurement error), and conditional inference with outcome prediction noise (Sui et al., arXiv:2603.05575 PPCI, which assumes the conditioning variable is correct). We prove that global correction can *increase* CATE bias relative to uncorrected estimates when misclassification and CATE heterogeneity are positively correlated.\\n\\n2. **Actionable diagnostic criterion (Bias Bound).** We derive a finite-sample bound: the bias of globally-corrected CATE estimates is proportional to the product of (a) misclassification heterogeneity across subgroups and (b) CATE heterogeneity across subgroups. This product yields a practical diagnostic: given pilot data, any researcher can compute this quantity *before* running full CATE analysis to determine whether global correction suffices or stratified correction is necessary. This diagnostic has standalone value independent of the EC-HTE framework—it applies to any CATE analysis using LLM-annotated effect modifiers. The bound implies that bias is worst when annotation is least accurate for subgroups where treatment effects are largest—empirically common (Ashwin et al., 2025).\\n\\n3. **EC-HTE: A three-stage correction framework** providing subgroup-specific misclassification correction, valid confidence intervals, and optimal annotation allocation (detailed below).\\n\\n4. **Empirical validation** via semi-synthetic benchmarks with controlled DGPs (including systematic variation of misclassification-CATE correlation direction) and a real-data misclassification pattern validation (Ashwin et al., 2025, Rohingya interview corpus).\\n\\n## Why Not Just Use Expert Labels?\\n\\nA natural objection: why not skip LLM annotation entirely and estimate CATEs using only expert-labeled data? The answer is a bias-variance tradeoff specific to CATE estimation. With a budget of ~2,000 expert labels distributed across k subgroups, each subgroup contains ~2,000/k observations. Honest causal forests (Wager & Athey, JASA 2018) split this further into estimation and splitting samples, leaving each cell with ~1,000/k observations—insufficient for detecting moderate heterogeneity when k ≥ 4.\\n\\nEC-HTE resolves this by using the full unlabeled corpus (potentially hundreds of thousands of LLM-annotated observations) for low-variance CATE estimation, with expert labels only for calibrating subgroup-specific misclassification. This extends the PPI/DSL paradigm—combining noisy large-scale with clean small-scale data—from ATE to CATE, where data demands make the combination even more critical.\\n\\n## Method: Three-Stage Framework\\n\\n**Stage 1: Hierarchical Bayesian Subgroup-Specific Misclassification Estimation.** For each effect-modifier subgroup g ∈ {1, …, k}, we estimate a confusion matrix C_g between LLM annotation and expert gold standard using a hierarchical Bayesian model with partial pooling. The hierarchy allows low-sample subgroups to borrow strength from the global structure while permitting genuine cross-subgroup variation. This addresses a key tension: per-subgroup independent estimation (high variance when subgroup expert-label counts are small) vs. single global estimation (biased when misclassification rates genuinely differ). For computational scalability in large-scale simulation studies, we use variational inference (VI) as the primary fitting method, with MCMC validation on representative configurations in the appendix.\\n\\n**Stage 2: Corrected CATE Estimation with Sandwich Variance Adjustment.** Corrected effect-modifier assignments are fed into an honest causal forest (Wager & Athey, JASA 2018). Because Stage 1 introduces nuisance parameter uncertainty, naive confidence intervals will under-cover. We derive a sandwich variance inflator propagating Stage 1 uncertainty into Stage 2 CIs, ensuring valid coverage. Bootstrap-based CIs serve as a robustness check.\\n\\n**Stage 3: Adaptive Expert Annotation Allocation.** Given a fixed expert budget B, we optimize allocation across subgroups by jointly minimizing misclassification estimation uncertainty and CATE estimation variance. The allocation criterion targets subgroups where the product of misclassification uncertainty and CATE magnitude is largest—precisely where annotation errors cause the most CATE distortion (as predicted by the bias bound).\\n\\n**Cold-start protocol.** Since Stage 3 requires initial estimates of both misclassification rates and CATE magnitudes, we employ a multi-batch protocol: (a) allocate 20–30% of B as a uniform pilot batch; (b) estimate initial confusion matrices and CATE values; (c) allocate remaining budget adaptively. We include pilot ratio sensitivity analysis (10%, 20%, 30%) in the experiments.\\n\\n## Related Work and Differentiation\\n\\n**Thread 1: LLM annotation debiasing (ATE-level).** DSL (Egami et al., NeurIPS 2023), PPI and its algebraic equivalence to survey-sampling difference estimators (Mozer, arXiv:2603.19160), active inference (Zrnic & Candès, ICML 2024), stratified PPI (StratPPI, Fisch et al., NeurIPS 2024), active multi-predictor PPI (AM-PPI, arXiv:2605.08429), and benchmarking thereof (Audinet de Pieuchon et al., EMNLP 2025). All locked to population-level estimands. Messing (arXiv:2604.11581) diagnoses hidden measurement error causing 40–60% CI under-coverage but does not extend to causal HTE correction.\\n\\n**Thread 2: HTE/CATE estimation methods.** Honest causal forests (Wager & Athey, JASA 2018), DR-learner, CATE-to-GATE aggregation bias correction (Persson et al., arXiv:2602.20383, shrinkage for aggregation-induced bias assuming clean covariates), and assumption-lean HTE detection under mismeasured modifiers (Boileau et al., arXiv:2512.03254, hypothesis testing for whether heterogeneity exists, not CATE estimation—complementary to EC-HTE). CATE evaluation standards established in \\\"Really Doing Great at Estimating CATE?\\\" (NeurIPS D&B 2021).\\n\\n**Thread 3: Conditional inference with ML predictions.** PPCI (Sui et al., arXiv:2603.05575) extends PPI to conditional functionals at fixed test points via kernel localization. Critical distinction: PPCI addresses outcome prediction noise at a correctly-specified conditioning point; EC-HTE addresses the situation where the conditioning variable (effect modifier) itself is misclassified. PPCI's localization anchors on correct covariate values—when the anchor itself is wrong (categorical misclassification), kernel localization cannot help.\\n\\n**Thread 4: Measurement error correction for ML-generated variables.** ForestIV (Bu et al., INFORMS JDS 2023) uses random forest tree tuples as instrumental variables, but requires IV exclusion restrictions and is limited to regression settings, not CATE with categorical effect modifiers. Jerzak et al. (arXiv:2509.12416, SRI framework) provide valid inference under noisy annotation but do not address effect modifier misclassification's impact on HTE. SIMEX handles continuous measurement error models but does not directly apply to categorical misclassification.\\n\\n**Thread 5: Causal inference with textual variables.** Feldman et al. (arXiv:2602.15730) address latent textual treatment estimation using SAE + residualization, assuming clean effect modifiers. Nwankwo et al. (arXiv:2502.10605, NeurIPS 2025 Workshop) optimize annotation allocation for doubly robust ATE with missing outcomes—different estimand (ATE vs. CATE), different error source (outcome missingness vs. effect modifier misclassification), different optimization landscape.\\n\\nEC-HTE sits at the intersection of Threads 1–4: correcting LLM misclassification specifically in effect modifiers for valid CATE estimation.\\n\\n## Validation Design\\n\\n### Semi-Synthetic Experiments\\n\\n**Corpus.** Political science text corpus with real-world linguistic variation (e.g., immigration framing data or partisan tweet datasets with known ideological annotations).\\n\\n**DGP Matrix.** Core factors:\\n- Misclassification severity: high (25–30%) vs. low (5–10%)\\n- CATE heterogeneity magnitude: large (>0.3 SD) vs. small (<0.1 SD)\\n- Subgroup count: k=3 vs. k=6\\n- **Misclassification-CATE correlation direction**: positive (high-error subgroups have high CATE, worst-case) vs. negative (high-error subgroups have low CATE, best-case)\\n\\nSelective design: 12–14 configurations covering all worst-case/best-case combinations for the key factors, rather than full 2⁴=16 factorial.\\n\\nTreatment assignment T ~ Bernoulli(0.5); potential outcomes Y(1), Y(0) generated with known ground-truth CATE τ(x) varying by subgroup.\\n\\n**Replications.** ≥ 200 per configuration.\\n\\n**Sample sizes.** N ∈ {2000, 5000, 10000}.\\n\\n**Expert label budget.** B ∈ {100, 500, 1000, 2000} to produce budget vs. performance curves. All methods compared under identical total budget.\\n\\n**LLMs.** Three annotators: GPT-4.1 (proprietary frontier), Qwen 3.5 (open-source large), Llama 3.3 (open-source, different family). Fourth LLM as appendix robustness check.\\n\\n### Comparison Methods (all under fixed expert annotation budget B)\\n\\n1. **Uncorrected**: LLM annotations used directly as effect modifiers in causal forest.\\n2. **Globally-corrected**: Single confusion matrix → corrected annotations → causal forest.\\n3. **Arm-specific (Mozer-style)**: Separate confusion matrices for treatment/control arms → causal forest.\\n4. **SIMEX + causal forest**: SIMEX-based measurement error correction → causal forest.\\n5. **Calibration-only + causal forest**: Platt scaling / temperature scaling → calibrated probabilities as soft effect modifiers → causal forest.\\n6. **EC-HTE (full)**: Hierarchical Bayesian subgroup-specific correction → sandwich-adjusted causal forest → adaptive allocation.\\n\\n### Evaluation Metrics\\n\\n- **PEHE**: √E[(τ̂(x) − τ(x))²], overall and per-subgroup.\\n- **Subgroup CATE bias**: |E[τ̂_g] − τ_g| for each subgroup g.\\n- **CI empirical coverage**: Fraction of replications where true CATE falls within 95% CI (target: 0.95).\\n- **CI width**: Efficiency measure at fixed coverage.\\n- **GATE RMSE**: RMSE of group average treatment effect estimates.\\n- **Bound tightness**: Observed bias vs. theoretical bound prediction.\\n\\n### Ablation Studies\\n\\n**Stage 1 ablation (partial pooling):**\\n- (a) Hierarchical Bayesian with VI (partial pooling)\\n- (b) Per-subgroup independent confusion matrices (no pooling)\\n- (c) Single global confusion matrix (full pooling)\\n- (d) Prior sensitivity: 2–3 prior settings (weakly informative, moderately informative, diffuse) on 1–2 representative DGP configurations\\n\\n**Stage 2 ablation (variance adjustment):**\\n- (a) Sandwich variance-adjusted CIs\\n- (b) Bootstrap-based CIs\\n- (c) Naive CIs (no adjustment)\\n→ Compare empirical coverage rates.\\n\\n**Stage 3 ablation (allocation strategy):**\\n- (a) Joint criterion (misclassification uncertainty × CATE magnitude)\\n- (b) Misclassification-uncertainty-only allocation\\n- (c) CATE-magnitude-only allocation\\n- (d) Uniform allocation\\n- (e) Pilot ratio sensitivity: 10%, 20%, 30% of B as uniform pilot batch\\n→ Compare PEHE at fixed total budget B.\\n\\n**Diagnostic validation:** Systematically vary misclassification heterogeneity and CATE heterogeneity (including correlation direction); produce heatmap of EC-HTE improvement over global correction vs. (product of heterogeneity measures). Verify that the theoretical bound predicts when global correction fails and when it suffices. Specifically demonstrate that global correction is worse than uncorrected in positive-correlation configurations.\\n\\n**CATE estimator robustness (appendix):** In 1–2 worst-case configurations, replace causal forest with DR-learner to verify EC-HTE's correction generalizes beyond a single CATE estimator.\\n\\n### Real-Data Application (Ashwin et al., 2025)\\n\\n**Dataset.** Rohingya refugee interview transcripts (Ashwin et al., Sociological Methods & Research 2025). This dataset's primary role is validating the premise of EC-HTE—that LLM misclassification is differential—rather than evaluating CATE estimation accuracy (the dataset lacks a natural causal structure).\\n\\n**Validation goals:**\\n1. **Differential misclassification pattern confirmation**: Quantify and visualize how LLM error rates vary across subgroups defined by refugee status, gender, education.\\n2. **Leave-expert-out calibration test**: Treat full human labels as ground truth, subsample B expert labels, evaluate whether EC-HTE's hierarchical confusion matrix estimates recover the true subgroup-specific error patterns better than global estimation.\\n3. **Diagnostic criterion demonstration**: Compute the misclassification-heterogeneity × CATE-heterogeneity product on this dataset (constructing a plausible causal question from available variables) to demonstrate the diagnostic's operability.\\n4. **Budget curve**: Accuracy of confusion matrix recovery vs. expert label budget B.\\n\\nCATE estimation accuracy is rigorously evaluated in the semi-synthetic experiments where ground-truth CATE is known.\\n\\n## Scope and Limitations\\n\\nEC-HTE targets **categorical effect modifiers** (stance, topic, sentiment, entity type, etc.)—the dominant use case when LLM annotations serve as moderators in computational social science. When the effect modifier is a continuous variable that has been discretized (binned), misclassification includes both LLM errors and discretization error; the current framework does not disentangle these sources. Extension to continuous effect modifiers with measurement error (where SIMEX-type approaches may be more natural) is future work.\\n\\n## Broader Applicability\\n\\nWhile our validation focuses on CSS, the same problem structure arises whenever ML-predicted categorical variables serve as effect modifiers: in medical research (ML-classified tumor subtypes as moderators of drug efficacy), education (NLP-coded classroom behaviors moderating intervention effects), and policy evaluation (automated classification of administrative characteristics). EC-HTE's framework and diagnostic criterion apply directly to these settings.\\n\\n## Expected Contributions\\n\\nEC-HTE provides (1) the first formal identification of measurement-error-induced CATE distortion in LLM-annotated pipelines, including the proof that global correction can worsen estimates; (2) an actionable diagnostic (the misclassification-heterogeneity × CATE-heterogeneity product) with standalone value for any CATE analysis; (3) a practical three-stage correction framework with valid inference; and (4) comprehensive empirical validation. The framework serves any study using LLM-annotated categorical text variables as effect modifiers in causal EC-HTE 的核心机制：当 subgroup misclassification rate 与真实 CATE magnitude 正相关时，pooled/global confusion-matrix correction 会比 uncorrected noisy modifier 产生更大的 subgroup CATE bias。\\\\n\\\\n具体产出：\\\\n1. 在本地项目目录创建可复现代码包，建议路径 `repro/exp_001_bias_bound_mvp/`，包含顶部文件注释和必要 docstring。\\\\n2. 实现半合成 DGP：k=3 或 k=4 subgroup，T~Bernoulli(0.5)，Y = base + tau_g*T + noise；真实 subgroup G 被 LLM/noisy modifier Z 按 subgroup-specific confusion/error rate 误分类。至少覆盖 positive-correlation 和 negative-correlation 两种配置。\\\\n3. 比较方法至少包含：uncorrected(Z direct)、global-corrected(pooled confusion matrix)、stratified/oracle subgroup-specific correction。可先用 GATE/subgroup CATE 的 plug-in estimator，若时间允许再加 causal forest/DR-learner接口占位。\\\\n4. 指标：subgroup CATE bias、GATE RMSE、global correction vs uncorrected 的 bias ratio、diagnostic product = misclassification heterogeneity × CATE heterogeneity。输出 CSV/JSON + 一张 heatmap 或 scatter 图。\\\\n5. 先 quick run：replications=10，确认脚本可启动；再正式小规模 run：replications>=200，N 至少覆盖 2000 和 5000，预算/混淆矩阵估计可先用 oracle known-confusion，另加有限 expert sample B=500 的估计版本作为可选。\\\\n6. 更新 registry：运行前/完成后保留 spec，包括 command、data_path='synthetic/generated_by_script'、data_count、model_name='semi_synthetic_gate_estimator'、seed、log_path、artifact paths；完成后根据结果设 conclusion=success/negative，并记录核心 metrics。若 registry DONE 转换要求 checkpoint_path，使用结果 JSON 路径作为 artifact/checkpoint 占位并说明非模型实验。\\\\n7. 注册 artifact：结果目录和简短报告。\\\\n\\\\n环境信息：本地 Python 环境未知，请先用 `python --version`、`pip list` 探测；只安装必要轻量包（numpy/pandas/scipy/matplotlib/seaborn/sklearn）。不要使用 SSH，不要使用 GPU。代码必须可在本地直接运行。\\\\n\\\\n验证步骤：\\\\n- `python repro/exp_001_bias_bound_mvp/run_experiment.py --help`\\\\n- `python 数学导致概率非法，clip 并在报告中说明；若 global correction 未劣于 uncorrected，检查 DGP 是否确实设置为 positive correlation 且 pooled correction 是否产生 under/over \\\"2026-05-31T15:44:37.508535+00:00\\\", \\\"role\\\": \\\"tool\\\", \\\"session\\\": \\\"feishu:group:oc_73eec441c03a72144a0c11d79028f43e:director\\\", \\\"text\\\": \\\"called mcp__agent_ml_research__update_roadmap\\\", \\\"tool\\\": \\\"mcp__agent_ml_research__update_roadmap\\\", \\\"tool_args\\\": {\\\"project\\\": \\\"ec_hte_codex_a\\\", \\\"reason\\\": \\\"初始化 EC-HTE internal research 路线图\\\", \\\"content\\\": \\\"# ROADMAP\\\\n\\\\n## 项目目标\\\\n\\\\nEC-HTE 的内部研究目标是验证并实现一套面向 LLM 分类 effect modifier 误分类的 HTE/CATE 纠偏流程。最终产出是技术报告、可复现实验包和可部署代码，不走会议论文提交流程。\\\\n\\\\n核心问题：当 LLM 注释误分类率在 subgroup 间不同，并且误分类强度与真实 CATE/HTE 结构相关时，使用单一全局 confusion matrix 的纠偏可能放大 subgroup CATE bias，甚至比不纠偏更差。\\\\n\\\\n## 当前主线 Hypothesis\\\\n\\\\nH1: 在 misclassification rate 与真实 subgroup CATE magnitude 正相关的 DGP 中，全局 confusion-matrix correction 会 under-correct 高误差/高效应 subgroup，并 over-correct 低误差/低效应 subgroup，因此 GATE RMSE 或 subgroup CATE bias 可能高于 uncorrected。\\\\n\\\\nH2: subgroup-specific correction 能降低 H1 暴露的结构性偏差；当 expert label budget 较小时，partial pooling 会比 per-subgroup independent confusion matrix 更稳健。\\\\n\\\\nH3: naive CI 会低估 Stage 1 misclassification estimation uncertainty；sandwich 或 bootstrap variance adjustment 能改善 subgroup CATE coverage。\\\\n\\\\nH4: adaptive expert annotation allocation 应优先投向 misclassification uncertainty 与 CATE magnitude 同时较高的 subgroup，在固定预算下优于 uniform allocation。\\\\n\\\\n## 短期计划\\\\n\\\\n1. 完成 `exp_001_bias_bound_mvp` CPU-only 半合成 MVP simulation，验证 H1，并输出 diagnostic product 与 observed bias 的关系图。\\\\n2. 基于 exp_001 结果决定是否进入 Stage 1：实现 global、per-subgroup independent、partial pooling 三类 confusion correction 的可复现实验脚本。\\\\n3. 建立统一评测协议：subgroup CATE bias、GATE RMSE、PEHE、CI coverage、CI width、bound tightness。所有指标必须能追溯到 registry exp_id。\\\\n4. 准备 GPU 实验骨架：固定 DGP matrix、replication 管理、artifact 输出格式、训练/评估命令模板；GPU 只使用项目白名单 `seetacloud-38274/root/cuda:0`。\\\\n5. 文献与 baseline 协议沉淀到 `memory.md` 的领域参考段，重点覆盖 CATE/HTE evaluation、PPI/DSL/StratPPI/Mozer setup、measurement-error correction。\\\\n\\\\n## 中期实验矩阵\\\\n\\\\nStage 0 机制验证：\\\\n- positive vs negative misclassification-CATE correlation\\\\n- high vs low misclassification heterogeneity\\\\n- high vs low CATE heterogeneity\\\\n- k=3 与 k=6 subgroup\\\\n\\\\nStage 1 correction：\\\\n- uncorrected\\\\n- global confusion correction\\\\n- arm-specific correction\\\\n- per-subgroup independent correction\\\\n- hierarchical/partial-pooling correction\\\\n\\\\nStage 2 inference：\\\\n- naive CI\\\\n- sandwich-adjusted CI\\\\n- bootstrap CI\\\\n- empirical coverage 与 CI width 对比\\\\n\\\\nStage 3 allocation：\\\\n- uniform allocation\\\\n- misclassification-uncertainty-only\\\\n- CATE-magnitude-only\\\\n- joint criterion\\\\n- pilot ratio 10%、20%、30%\\\\n\\\\nReal-data validation：\\\\n- Rohingya interview corpus 上验证 LLM misclassification 是否随 refugee status、gender、education 差异化变化\\\\n- leave-expert-out calibration test\\\\n- diagnostic criterion operability demonstration\\\\n\\\\n## 工程产出\\\\n\\\\n- `scripts/`：可复现实验入口，包含 DGP、correction、estimation、evaluation。\\\\n- `configs/`：每个实验的 YAML/JSON 配置，记录 seed、DGP 参数、预算、method。\\\\n- `artifacts/`：实验表格、图、日志、registry 可追溯输出。\\\\n- `docs/`：技术报告素材与复现说明，由 Director 汇总。\\\\n- 部署代码：提供 diagnostic calculator 和 correction workflow 的最小可调用接口。\\\\n\\\\n## 决策规则\\\\n\\\\n- 任何负向结果必须先汇报 Director，不得默默跑同一方向的下一步。\\\\n- 如果 exp_001 未能复现 global correction worse than uncorrected，优先诊断 DGP、correction algebra 和 metric 定义，而不是直接推进 Stage 1。\\\\n- GPU 实验启动前必须确认项目白名单 GPU 可见，并记录 server、GPU 型号、driver、命令、数据路径、seed。\\\\n- 所有定量结论只引用 registry 中有 exp_id 的结果。\\\"}}\\n/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_11/projects/ec_hte_codex_a/logs/chats/feishu_group_oc_6ec727e1e725f2b753e5fd3ea4892bd3_reviewer.jsonl:7:{\\\"ts\\\": Artifact\\n\\nCreated: 2026-05-31 UTC\\nProject: ec_hte_codex_a\\nType: research protocol\\n\\n## Scope\\n\\nThis artifact summarizes literature-backed evaluation choices and baseline boundaries for first-batch EC-HTE experiments. The durable copy is also written to `memory.md` under `## 领域参考` and `## 首批实验协议`.\\n\\n## Key Protocol Decisions\\n\\n- HTE/CATE evaluation must report PEHE, per-subgroup CATE bias, GATE RMSE, empirical 95% CI coverage, CI width, and EC-HTE bound diagnostic product.\\n- Formal semi-synthetic experiments follow PROJECT_BRIEF.md: `R >= 200`, `N in {2000, 5000, 10000}`, `B in {100, 500, 1000, 2000}`, `k in {3, 6}`, `T ~ Bernoulli(0.5)`.\\n- Smoke/MVP runs may be smaller, but must be marked as engineering checks and excluded from quantitative claims.\\n- PPI/DSL/StratPPI/ASI/AM-PPI are valid baselines for population-level estimands or allocation, not direct fixes for categorical effect-modifier misclassification unless subgroup membership error is explicitly modeled.\\n- Mozer-style arm-specific rectification is an arm-level residual/confusion correction baseline; failure on CATE/GATE should be described as a mismatch to modifier-misclassification structure, not as a broad invalidation of PPI.\\n- Real-data Rohingya validation only tests differential LLM misclassification and confusion-matrix recovery; it does not establish true CATE accuracy.\\n\\n## Baseline Matrix\\n\\n| Experiment | Metrics | Baselines | Minimum formal scale | Cost note |\\n|---|---|---|---|---|\\n| MVP / Stage 0 bias-bound mechanism | subgroup CATE bias, GATE RMSE, diagnostic product, optional PEHE | uncorrected, global confusion, oracle/stratified correction | `N=2000`, `k=3`, `R>=200` | CPU-only low |\\n| Stage 1 partial pooling | confusion recovery, PEHE, GATE RMSE, subgroup bias, budget curve | global, per-subgroup independent, hierarchical partial pooling, oracle | `N in {2000,5000}`, `B in {100,500,1000,2000}`, `k in {3,6}`, `R>=200` | CPU medium; \\\"/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_11/projects/ec_hte_codex_a/ROADMAP.md\\\", \\\"content\\\": \\\"# ROADMAP\\\\n\\\\n> 最后更新: 2026-05-31 15:53 UTC | 更新原因: 量化 H1/exp_002 判据并新增 H2 partial-pooling claim\\\\n\\\\n## 项目目标\\\\n\\\\nEC-HTE 的内部研究目标是验证并实现一套面向 LLM 分类 effect modifier 误分类的 HTE/CATE 纠偏流程。最终产出是技术报告、可复现实验包和可部署代码，不走会议论文提交流程。\\\\n\\\\n核心问题：当 LLM 注释误分类率在 subgroup 间不同，并且误分类强度与真实 CATE/HTE 结构相关时，使用单一全局 confusion matrix 的纠偏可能放大 subgroup CATE bias，甚至比不纠偏更差。\\\\n\\\\n## 当前主线 Hypothesis\\\\n\\\\nH1: 在 misclassification rate 与真实 subgroup CATE magnitude 正相关的 DGP 中，全局 confusion-matrix correction 会 under-correct 高误差/高效应 subgroup，并 over-correct 低误差/低效应 subgroup，因此 GATE RMSE 可能高于 uncorrected。\\\\n\\\\nH2: subgroup-specific correction 能降低 H1 暴露的结构性偏差；当 expert label budget 较小时，partial pooling 会比 per-subgroup independent confusion matrix 更稳健。\\\\n\\\\nH3: naive CI 会低估 Stage 1 misclassification estimation uncertainty；sandwich 或 bootstrap variance adjustment 能改善 subgroup CATE coverage。\\\\n\\\\nH4: adaptive expert annotation allocation 应优先投向 misclassification uncertainty 与 CATE magnitude 同时较高的 subgroup，在固定预算下优于 uniform allocation。\\\\n\\\\n## Operational 判据\\\\n\\\\n`exp_001_bias_bound_mvp`：唯一 primary metric 是 GATE RMSE；mean subgroup CATE bias 与 max subgroup CATE bias 是 secondary diagnostics；最低 200 replications。\\\\n\\\\nH1 success for exp_001：在 positive-correlation high-misclassification/high-CATE-heterogeneity 设置中，`oracle_global - uncorrected` 的 GATE RMSE 差值大于 0，且 bootstrap 95% CI lower bound > 0。`estimated_global` 单独报告；`estimated_global - oracle_global` 用于量化 finite-sample confusion-estimation noise。如果 GATE RMSE 支持 H1 但 secondary bias diagnostics 方向相反，标记 mixed，先 Director 复核再推进 Stage 1。\\\\n\\\\nH1 negative for exp_001：如果 positive-correlation worst-case 中 oracle global 不劣于 uncorrected，则 H1 暂不支持；下一步先审计 DGP construction、correction algebra、metric definitions、subgroup weighting，而不是推进 Stage 1。\\\\n\\\\n`exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid`：primary metric 是 `global_worse_rate_difference_positive_minus_negative`；secondary metrics 是 `bias_bound_vs_global\\n…[truncated 3690 chars]\", \"tool\": {\\\"path\\\": \\\"/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_11/projects/ec_hte_codex_a/ROADMAP.md\\\", \\\"content\\\": \\\"# ROADMAP\\\\n\\\\n> 最后更新: 2026-05-31 15:53 UTC | 更新原因: 量化 H1/exp_002 判据并新增 H2 partial-pooling claim\\\\n\\\\n## 项目目标\\\\n\\\\nEC-HTE 的内部研究目标是验证并实现一套面向 LLM 分类 effect modifier 误分类的 HTE/CATE 纠偏流程。最终产出是技术报告、可复现实验包和可部署代码，不走会议论文提交流程。\\\\n\\\\n核心问题：当 LLM 注释误分类率在 subgroup 间不同，并且误分类强度与真实 CATE/HTE 结构相关时，使用单一全局 confusion matrix 的纠偏可能放大 subgroup CATE bias，甚至比不纠偏更差。\\\\n\\\\n## 当前主线 Hypothesis\\\\n\\\\nH1: 在 misclassification rate 与真实 subgroup CATE magnitude 正相关的 DGP 中，全局 confusion-matrix correction 会 under-correct 高误差/高效应 subgroup，并 over-correct 低误差/低效应 subgroup，因此 GATE RMSE 可能高于 uncorrected。\\\\n\\\\nH2: subgroup-specific correction 能降低 H1 暴露的结构性偏差；当 expert label budget 较小时，partial pooling 会比 per-subgroup independent confusion matrix 更稳健。\\\\n\\\\nH3: naive CI 会低估 Stage 1 misclassification estimation uncertainty；sandwich 或 bootstrap variance adjustment 能改善 subgroup CATE coverage。\\\\n\\\\nH4: adaptive expert annotation allocation 应优先投向 misclassification uncertainty 与 CATE magnitude 同时较高的 subgroup，在固定预算下优于 uniform allocation。\\\\n\\\\n## Operational 判据\\\\n\\\\n`exp_001_bias_bound_mvp`：唯一 primary metric 是 GATE RMSE；mean subgroup CATE bias 与 max subgroup CATE bias 是 secondary diagnostics；最低 200 replications。\\\\n\\\\nH1 success for exp_001：在 positive-correlation high-misclassification/high-CATE-heterogeneity 设置中，`oracle_global - uncorrected` 的 GATE RMSE 差值大于 0，且 bootstrap 95% CI lower bound > 0。`estimated_global` 单独报告；`estimated_global - oracle_global` 用于量化 finite-sample confusion-estimation noise。如果 GATE RMSE 支持 H1 但 secondary bias diagnostics 方向相反，标记 mixed，先 Director 复核再推进 Stage 1。\\\\n\\\\nH1 negative for exp_001：如果 positive-correlation worst-case 中 oracle global 不劣于 uncorrected，则 H1 暂不支持；下一步先审计 DGP construction、correction algebra、metric definitions、subgroup weighting，而不是推进 Stage 1。\\\\n\\\\n`exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid`：primary metric 是 `global_worse_rate_difference_positive_minus_negative`；secondary metrics 是 `bias_bound_vs_global\\n…[truncated 3690 chars]\", \"tool\": \\\"/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_11/projects/ec_hte_codex_a/memory.md\\\", \\\"content\\\": \\\"# Memory\\\\n\\\\n## 项目定位\\\\nEC-HTE 研究目标是纠正 LLM 生成的分类 effect modifier 中的差异性误分类对 CATE/HTE 估计造成的结构性偏差。首要可证伪机制是：当 subgroup misclassification rate 与真实 subgroup CATE magnitude 正相关时，单一全局 confusion-matrix correction 会系统性 under-correct 高误差/高效应 subgroup，并 over-correct 低误差/低效应 subgroup，因此可能比 uncorrected 更差。\\\\n\\\\n## 执行约束\\\\n`PROJECT_BRIEF.md` 同步存在于本项目 `docs/PROJECT_BRIEF.md`。项目配置绑定 `seetacloud-38274/root/cuda:0`；2026-05-31 Director 已确认 `list_available_gpus` 可见该 GPU，型号为 NVIDIA RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell Server Edition，显存约 98GB。GPU 实验仍必须只使用白名单 `cuda:0`，并在启动前记录 server、GPU 型号、driver、命令、数据路径与 seed。\\\\n\\\\n## 领域参考\\\\n\\\\n### HTE/CATE 标准评测协议\\\\n\\\\n- Honest causal forest: Wager & Athey (JASA 2018; arXiv:1510.04342, https://arxiv.org/abs/1510.04342; DOI page https://www.tandfonline.com/doi/abs/10.1080/01621459.2017.1319839) 给出用于 CATE/HTE 的 causal forest 和推断框架；honesty/sample splitting 是其核心推断条件。EC-HTE 的 causal-forest baseline 应固定 honesty 开启，并报告 CI coverage 与 CI width，避免只报点估计误差。\\\\n- GRF implementation reference: `grf::causal_forest` 文档说明 causal forest 用于估计 tau(X)，并提示 accurate confidence intervals 通常需要比 prediction 更多 trees；同时把 honesty 定义为 subsample splitting。来源：https://grf-labs.github.io/grf/reference/causal_forest.html。实现时需记录 `num.trees`、`honesty`、`honesty.fraction`、seed、train/test split。\\\\n- CATE benchmark caution: Curth, Svensson, Weatherall & van der Schaar, \\\\\\\"Really Doing Great at Estimating CATE?\\\\\\\" NeurIPS D&B 2021（https://openreview.net/forum?id=FQLzQqGEAH；PDF https://datasets-benchmarks-proceedings.neurips.cc/paper_files/paper/2021/file/2a79ea27c279e471f4d180b08d62b00a-Paper-round2.pdf）指出常见 semi-synthetic CATE benchmark 和单一 metric 可能偏向特定算法，因此 EC-HTE 必须同时报告 pointwise、group-level 和 inference metrics，而不是只报告 PEHE。\\\\n- PEHE / root-PEHE: Shalit, Johansson & Sontag, ICML 2017（https://proceedings.mlr.press/v70/shalit17a.html；arXiv:1606.03976）沿用 ITE/CATE 估计误差评测；本项目定义为 `sqrt(mean_i((tau_hat\\n…[truncated 10565 chars]\", \"tool\": -lc \\\"sed -n '1,220p' /home/ubuntu/agent-ml-research/skills/global/ml-experiment-guide.skill.md\\\"\",\"aggregated_output\":\"---\\ndescription: 跨项目 ML 实验避坑指南——从多个项目沉淀的通用踩坑经验与最佳实践。当 Worker 准备写实验代码、或排查实验问题时使用。\\nname: ml-experiment-guide\\nroles:\\n- worker\\n- reviewer\\n- director\\nscope: global\\ntags:\\n- experiment\\n- troubleshooting\\n- best-practice\\nversion: 72\\n---\\n\\n# ML 实验避坑指南\\n\\n本指南由各项目 Director 持续维护，记录跨项目通用的实验教训。每条只保留问题模式和规则，不含项目/实验特定信息。\\n\\n## Eval 规范\\n### 必须全量全类别 eval，partial 数据严禁混用\\n- **问题模式**: 只用部分类别/样本做 mid-eval，与全量 baseline 比较导致错误决策；或 partial eval 报告的指标与全量 eval 结果混用，误判趋势\\n- **规则**: 所有 eval 必须覆盖完整测试集；partial eval 必须标注 `PARTIAL: X/Y samples`，禁止与全量结果直接比较；partial 和全量数据必须分开记录和比较，partial 数据禁止用于最终决策\\n\\n### 长训练必须有 mid-training eval + 持久化\\n- **问题模式**: 训练 >3000 步无中间 eval，复现失败时无同步点对照，根因诊断退化为猜测\\n- **规则**: 任何长训练（>3000 steps）必须每 1000 step 至少一次 val eval；eval 数据保留到 JSON/CSV（不仅是日志），确保复现实验时有逐 step 对照基线\\n\\n### 相关性评估必须用全量数据或 bootstrap CI\\n- **问题模式**: 用少量随机采样（如 200 对）计算 Spearman/Pearson 相关系数，因采样方差导致 ρ 被严重高估（实测案例：200 对 ρ=0.57，全量 8840 对 ρ=0.31，高估近一倍），基于此做出错误的 PROCEED 决策\\n- **规则**: 相关性评估必须用全量数据；若因计算成本必须采样，使用分层抽样 + bootstrap CI（≥1000 次重采样）报告置信区间，不能用点估计做 go/no-go 决策\\n\\n### Retrieval 评估：metric 定义 + sanity check\\n- **问题模式**: (1) 引用 SOTA 数字时混淆 R@1/R@10/R_subset@K 或不同 candidate pool 规模，导致误判自己的结果异常；(2) eval 脚本的 candidate pool 构造错误（target 未排除、query 字段错用、split 泄漏）导致虚高指标\\n- **规则**: 引用 SOTA 数字时必须带 metric 名 + candidate pool 规模 + 数据集名；评估前先用 5-10 条 case 比对论文公开数字（同一 metric 同一 pool），差距 >2x 才需要停下排查\\n\\n### Decoder-only 模型批量 eval 必须用 left-padding\\n- **问题模式**: 批量推理/eval 时 tokenizer 使用默认 right-padding，decoder-only 架构下 PAD token 出现在输入末尾，破坏 causal attention mask，系统性降低生成质量。实测导致 eval 指标被低估 5-8pp，且 DDP multi-GPU eval 放大此效应（更大 batch = 更多 padding）\\n- **规则**: 任何 decoder-only 模型（GPT、LLaMA、Qwen 等）的批量生成/eval 前必须设置 `tokenizer.padding_side = 'left'`；运行时如果出现 `right-padding was detected` 警告必须立即修复，不能忽略\\n\\n## Checkpoint 管理\\n### save_total_limit + save_best_model\\n- **问题模式**: save_total_limit 过小，训练继续运行后最优 checkpoint 被自动删除，增益全部丧失\\n- **规则**: save_total_limit ≥ 50；必须配置 save_best_model 将最优 checkpoint 备份到独立目录（永不自动删除）；配置 early_stopping_patience\\n\\n## 进程管理\\n### Kill 训练进程必须杀整个进程组\\n- **问题模式**: (1) kill 主进程后，accelerate/DDP 的 worker 子进程继续运行，训练实际未停止；(2) tmux/nohup 启动的训练中，kill bash 包裹进程（tmux → bash PID）但 SIGKILL 未传递给 python 子进程，python 继续占 GPU 成为僵尸进程，而监控误报 GPU 空闲\\n- **规则**: kill 时必须用 `kill -- -&lt;pgid>` 杀整个进程组，或 `kill $(pgrep -P $PID)` 先杀子进程再杀父进程。kill 后必须 `nvidia-smi` 确认目标 GPU 上无残留进程。绝不能只 kill tmux/bash 父 PID 就假设训练已停止\\n\\n### 标记实验 DONE 时必须同步 kill 进程\\n- **问题模式**: Agent 在 registry 中标记实验 DONE（success/failed）但未实际 kill 训练进程，进程继续占用 GPU 跑废模型（如崩溃后 1% accuracy），report.md 误报\\\"GPU 空闲\\\"而实际被僵尸进程占满，排队实验无法启动\\n- **规则**: 标记实验 DONE 的操作必须原子化：(1) kill 进程 (2) nvidia-smi 确认 GPU 释放 (3) 更新 registry。三步全做完才算 DONE。绝不接受\\\"先标 DONE 再说\\\"\\n\\n### 训练脚本必须有 collapse 早停机制\\n- **问题模式**: 训练过程中 val_acc 崩溃（如从 50%+ 跌到 1%），但脚本无检测逻辑，继续运行数小时浪费 GPU，直到人工发现才 kill\\n- **规则**: 训练脚本必须实现 collapse 检测：val_acc 相对历史最高值下降超过 30pp 且连续 3 个 eval 不恢复则自动终止训练，退出码设为非零以便监控系统识别\\n\\n### 实验状态检测禁止依赖 tmux session 状态，必须用 ps -p PID\\n- **问题模式**: Agent 通过 tmux session 是否存在来判断训练进程是否仍在运行。tmux session 可因多种原因消失（server 崩溃、手动 kill-session、session 超时清理）而训练 python 进程仍在正常运行。反复导致正在跑的实验被误标为 failed/killed，registry 与实际状态严重不一致，report.md 误报 GPU 空闲\\n- **规则**: 检测实验是否仍在运行时，必须用 `ps -p &lt;PID> -o pid=` 直接查进程存活状态，辅以 `nvidia-smi` 确认 GPU 占用。禁止以 tmux session 存在与否作为进程存活的判断依据。状态检测与操作前验证同等重要——错误的状态检测会级联导致错误的 registry 更新、错误的资源报告、错误的实验调度\\n\\n### 操作进程前必须 SSH 验证实际状态\\n- **问题模式**: (1) 提交重跑时未验证旧进程是否已死、目标 GPU 是否被占用，导致新旧进程冲突；(2) registry/report 标记实验为 failed/orphan，Agent 未验证进程实际状态就直接 kill，误杀正常训练中的进程（进程实际在正常出指标）；(3) 实验因数据路径错误失败后未验证数据是否存在就盲目重试\\n- **规则**: 对进程执行任何操作（kill、重提交、标记状态）前，必须 ssh_read_only 验证：(1) `ps -p &lt;PID>` 确认进程实际存活状态 (2) `nvidia-smi` 确认目标 GPU 占用情况 (3) 关键输入路径存在。**元数据（registry/report/cron 报告）不能替代实际状态验证**——registry 说 failed 不等于进程已死，report 说空闲不等于 GPU 真空闲。验证全通过才能操作\\n\\n### 训练提交必须走 job submission 系统，禁止手动绕过\\n- **问题模式**: 通过手动 tmux/nohup 直接 `python train.py` 绕过 job submission 系统（如 `submit_training_job`），导致：(1) 环境未正确初始化（conda activate 未执行、工作目录错误），训练 silent failure 或提前退出但 exit_code=0；(2) 日志未对接监控 hook，完成事件无法正确触发或被旧残留日志误触发；(3) cron 监控无法追踪真实完成时间和状态\\n- **规则**: 所有训练实验必须通过 job submission 系统提交（自动处理环境激活、目录切换、日志 tee、监控注册）。禁止手动 tmux/nohup 直接启动训练脚本。手动修复场景（如 kill 后重提交）也必须通过 submission 系统，不能为了\\\"快\\\"而绕过\\n\\n### 高频迭代库 API 启动前 smoke test\\n- **问题模式**: vLLM/transformers/peft 等高频迭代库的 API 在脚本写好到执行之间漂移（如 vLLM 0.19 移除 `LLM(device=...)`、transformers Trainer 参数重命名、peft 接口签名变更），nohup 后才崩溃，浪费一次完整 launcher 流程 + GPU 占位 + cron 周期\\n- **规则**: worker 在 nohup 启动前必须先跑 `python -c \\\"import lib; print(lib.__version__); from lib import 关键类; import inspect; print(inspect.signature(关键类.__init__))\\\"` 验签，把签名与脚本里的调用比对。不一致就先 patch 脚本再启动，签名输出和 patch diff 都写入 registry.notes。绝不接受\\\"先启动看看\\\"\\n\\n## 消融实验\\n### 消融脚本必须与 baseline 逐项对齐 augmentation/regularization\\n- **问题模式**: 消融实验（ablation）从模板或新建脚本创建时，augmentation 和 regularization 参数（mixup, cutmix, label_smoothing, drop_path, weight_decay 等）与 baseline 不一致。消融变量的效果被 augmentation 差异淹没，产生数个甚至十几个 pp 的虚假差异，且过拟合模式（train-val gap）完全吻合 augmentation 差异而非消融变量\\n- **规则**: 创建消融脚本时，必须从 baseline 脚本复制并仅修改消融目标参数；提交前逐行 diff 确认只有消融变量不同；提交审批时附上 baseline 和消融脚本的 augmentation 参数对照表。Reviewer 审查消融实验时必须交叉核对 augmentation 设置\\n\\n## 资源与性能\\n### OOM 处理优先级\\n- **问题模式**: GPU 显存不足导致训练崩溃\\n- **规则**: 优先降 batch_size + 调 gradient_accumulation_steps 保持等效 batch；其次降 max_length / 换更小模型\\n\\n### 训练脚本禁止硬编码 CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES\\n- **问题模式**: 训练脚本内部硬编码 `os.environ[\\\"CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES\\\"] = \\\"0,1\\\"` 等语句，覆盖 job scheduler/launcher 通过环境变量设置的 GPU 隔离。导致进程实际跑到非预期 GPU 上，多实验在同一物理 GPU 上冲突，而 scheduler 层面无感知——监控报告 GPU 分配正确但实际已冲突\\n- **规则**: GPU 分配由 job scheduler/launcher 统一管理（通过启动时设 CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES 环境变量）。训练脚本内部禁止覆盖 CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES。如需限制 GPU 范围，在 launcher 配置或提交命令中设置，不在 .py 文件中硬编码。Code review 时 `grep -r \\\"CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES\\\" *.py` 发现硬编码必须移除\\n\\n## 决策纪律\\n### 数据/eval 错误优先于技术结论\\n- **问题模式**: 因 eval 数据错误（坑 #eval）得出\\\"方法无效\\\"的技术结论（如误判为 reward hacking），导致过早放弃有效方向\\n- **规则**: 诊断实验问题时，必须先排除 eval/数据层面的错误，确认数据可靠后再下技术结论\\n\\n### 门控/警戒线触发后必须执行，不依赖主观判断\\n- **问题模式**: 设定了明确的数值门控（如\\\"avg R@10 < 17% 则暂停\\\"），但 eval 触发后因\\\"涨了一点\\\"\\\"趋势向好\\\"等 confirmation bias 继续放行，忽视绝对值与门控的差距，浪费 GPU 时间\\n- **规则**: 每次 eval 必须先与门控/警戒线比较，触发即执行预定动作（暂停/排查/kill），不允许\\\"再等一个 eval 看看\\\"；讨论趋势只在门控未触发时有意义\\n\\n### 复现实验必须确认纯净启动\\n- **问题模式**: 复现失败诊断中遗漏启动命令含 --resume-from-checkpoint，所有分析假设从 step 0 开始，optimizer/scheduler/sampler 状态污染被忽视，导致根因定位偏离数小时\\n- **规则**: 复现失败诊断的第一条必查项：启动命令是否含 --resume / --pretrained / --init-from 等非纯净启动参数；若有 resume，必须追溯 source checkpoint 的完整来源（何时生成、用什么配置、是否完整训练产物）\\n\\n### 重训/复现必须显式验证所有关键超参\\n- **问题模式**: 重训实验声称\\\"identical config\\\"，但使用了不同脚本/不同默认值，关键超参（如 gradient_accumulation_steps）静默漂移，导致 effective batch size 差一倍，性能差距数个百分点，排查耗时数小时\\n- **规则**: 重训/复现启动前必须 (1) 显式打印所有关键超参到日志首行（batch_size, grad_accum, lr, warmup, max_steps, LoRA config）(2) 与原实验日志逐项比对 (3) 不同脚本间不能假设默认值相同——必须显式传参覆盖\\n\\n## 持久化与解耦\\n### generation 与 evaluation 必须解耦持久化\\n\\n- **问题模式**: ML 评估 pipeline 把 generation 与 evaluation 写在同一个 long-running 脚本里，eval 库崩溃导致数小时 GPU 生成丢失\\n- **规则**: 任何 generation pipeline 必须在生成阶段结束前持久化 manifest + sample 文件到磁盘，evaluation 走独立可重入入口读盘，不允许 in-memory pass-through\\n\\n## 训练 log 卫生\\n### 训练 log 文件追加共享导致历史抽取错乱\\n\\n**问题模式**：同一 log 文件被多次训练进程追加共享时，旧进程残留的 stdout 行会污染后续 grep 抽取。\\n\\n**规则**：\\n1. 训练每次启动用新 log 文件（带 timestamp/PID 后缀）\\n2. 任何 eval/best 指标读取必须从 callback 写的 JSON 文件读，禁止 grep 训练日志文本\\n3. callback 写 JSON 若是 OVERWRITE 模式，resume 必须先 load 历史 history/best\\n\\n## 模型版本\\n### 使用外部模型（API 或开源）前必须验证版本时效性\\n- **问题模式**: 代码中使用过时的模型 ID（如 gpt-4o、claude-3-5-sonnet、gemini-1.5-pro、Llama-3.1），这些模型可能已退役、降级、或有性价比显著更优的替代。Agent 的训练数据天然滞后，凭记忆选模型大概率选到旧版\\n- **规则**: 首次在项目中调用某厂商的 LLM API 或选用某系列开源模型时，必须 WebSearch 确认当前最新可用版本和推荐型号，将结果记入 memory.md（厂商 + 型号 + 确认日期）。后续同项目可参考 memory.md 中已验证的版本，但注意这只是历史验证记录、不是白名单——可能已过时或有误，有疑问时重新 WebSearch 确认。例外：用户或实验需求明确指定模型版本（复现、对比、演进分析等）\\n\\n## 论文写作\\n### 论文数字必须从 eval 输出程序化提取\\n- **问题模式**: 手动从 eval 输出抄写数字到 LaTeX，导致系统性偏差——所有不一致都朝\\\"看起来更好\\\"方向偏移（如 ratio 从 >1 写成 <1 跨越分界线，accuracy 多舍少入）。confirmation bias 使错误不易被发现\\n- **规则**: 论文中的每个实验数字必须通过脚本从 eval JSON 输出文件直接提取并生成 LaTeX 宏/表格行。禁止手动抄写。修改数字后必须与源 JSON 逐一核对。Reviewer 审查论文数字时必须交叉比对 eval 输出文件\\n\",\"exit_code\":0,\"status\":\"completed\"}}\n./logs/workers/worker_gpu_exp_002_dgp_grid_launch_20260531T154505.log:23:{\"type\":\"item.completed\",\"item\":{\"id\":\"item_10\",\"type\":\"mcp_tool_call\",\"server\":\"agent-ml-research\",\"tool\":\"read_registry\",\"arguments\":{\"project\":\"ec_hte_codex_a\",\"limit\":10,\"fields\":[\"*\"]},\"result\":{\"content\":[{\"type\":\"text\",\"text\":\"{\\\"success\\\": {\\\"elapsed_seconds\\\": 0.003}}\"}},\"error\":null,\"status\":\"completed\"}}\n./docs/PROJECT_BRIEF.md:11:**Key anti-intuitive finding: global correction can actively worsen CATE estimates.** Consider a two-subgroup example. Subgroup A has high misclassification (25%) and large CATE (0.5 SD); Subgroup B has low misclassification (5%) and small CATE (0.1 SD). A global correction uses a pooled confusion matrix (~15% error), which under-corrects Subgroup A and over-corrects Subgroup B. The net effect: globally-corrected CATE estimates can have *larger* bias than uncorrected estimates. This is not an edge case—it occurs whenever the misclassification pattern and CATE structure are positively correlated, which empirical evidence suggests is common in practice.\n./docs/PROJECT_BRIEF.md:33:**Stage 1: Hierarchical Bayesian Subgroup-Specific Misclassification Estimation.** For each effect-modifier subgroup g ∈ {1, …, k}, we estimate a confusion matrix C_g between LLM annotation and expert gold standard using a hierarchical Bayesian model with partial pooling. The hierarchy allows low-sample subgroups to borrow strength from the global structure while permitting genuine cross-subgroup variation. This addresses a key tension: per-subgroup independent estimation (high variance when subgroup expert-label counts are small) vs. single global estimation (biased when misclassification rates genuinely differ). For computational scalability in large-scale simulation studies, we use variational inference (VI) as the primary fitting method, with MCMC validation on representative configurations in the appendix.\n./docs/PROJECT_BRIEF.md:51:**Thread 5: Causal inference with textual variables.** Feldman et al. (arXiv:2602.15730) address latent textual treatment estimation using SAE + residualization, assuming clean effect modifiers. Nwankwo et al. (arXiv:2502.10605, NeurIPS 2025 Workshop) optimize annotation allocation for doubly robust ATE with missing outcomes—different estimand (ATE vs. CATE), different error source (outcome missingness vs. effect modifier misclassification), different optimization landscape.\n./docs/PROJECT_BRIEF.md:61:**DGP Matrix.** Core factors:\n./docs/PROJECT_BRIEF.md:77:**LLMs.** Three annotators: GPT-4.1 (proprietary frontier), Qwen 3.5 (open-source large), Llama 3.3 (open-source, different family). Fourth LLM as appendix robustness check.\n./docs/PROJECT_BRIEF.md:82:2. **Globally-corrected**: Single confusion matrix → corrected annotations → causal forest.\n./docs/PROJECT_BRIEF.md:102:- (c) Single global confusion matrix (full pooling)\n./docs/PROJECT_BRIEF.md:129:2. **Leave-expert-out calibration test**: Treat full human labels as ground truth, subsample B expert labels, evaluate whether EC-HTE's hierarchical confusion matrix estimates recover the true subgroup-specific error patterns better than global estimation.\n./docs/PROJECT_BRIEF.md:131:4. **Budget curve**: Accuracy of confusion matrix recovery vs. expert label budget B.\n./docs/PROJECT_BRIEF.md:137:EC-HTE targets **categorical effect modifiers** (stance, topic, sentiment, entity type, etc.)—the dominant use case when LLM annotations serve as moderators in computational social science. When the effect modifier is a continuous variable that has been discretized (binned), misclassification includes both LLM errors and discretization error; the current framework does not disentangle these sources. Extension to continuous effect modifiers with measurement error (where SIMEX-type approaches may be more natural) is future work.\n./logs/workers/worker_code_plan_001_h1_cpu_mvp_gate_rmse_v3_20260531T161019.log:6:{\"type\":\"item.completed\",\"item\":{\"id\":\"item_1\",\"type\":\"command_execution\",\"command\":\"/bin/bash -lc \\\"sed -n '1,220p' /home/ubuntu/agent-ml-research/skills/global/ml-experiment-guide.skill.md\\\"\",\"aggregated_output\":\"---\\ndescription: 跨项目 ML 实验避坑指南——从多个项目沉淀的通用踩坑经验与最佳实践。当 Worker 准备写实验代码、或排查实验问题时使用。\\nname: ml-experiment-guide\\nroles:\\n- worker\\n- reviewer\\n- director\\nscope: global\\ntags:\\n- experiment\\n- troubleshooting\\n- best-practice\\nversion: 72\\n---\\n\\n# ML 实验避坑指南\\n\\n本指南由各项目 Director 持续维护，记录跨项目通用的实验教训。每条只保留问题模式和规则，不含项目/实验特定信息。\\n\\n## Eval 规范\\n### 必须全量全类别 eval，partial 数据严禁混用\\n- **问题模式**: 只用部分类别/样本做 mid-eval，与全量 baseline 比较导致错误决策；或 partial eval 报告的指标与全量 eval 结果混用，误判趋势\\n- **规则**: 所有 eval 必须覆盖完整测试集；partial eval 必须标注 `PARTIAL: X/Y samples`，禁止与全量结果直接比较；partial 和全量数据必须分开记录和比较，partial 数据禁止用于最终决策\\n\\n### 长训练必须有 mid-training eval + 持久化\\n- **问题模式**: 训练 >3000 步无中间 eval，复现失败时无同步点对照，根因诊断退化为猜测\\n- **规则**: 任何长训练（>3000 steps）必须每 1000 step 至少一次 val eval；eval 数据保留到 JSON/CSV（不仅是日志），确保复现实验时有逐 step 对照基线\\n\\n### 相关性评估必须用全量数据或 bootstrap CI\\n- **问题模式**: 用少量随机采样（如 200 对）计算 Spearman/Pearson 相关系数，因采样方差导致 ρ 被严重高估（实测案例：200 对 ρ=0.57，全量 8840 对 ρ=0.31，高估近一倍），基于此做出错误的 PROCEED 决策\\n- **规则**: 相关性评估必须用全量数据；若因计算成本必须采样，使用分层抽样 + bootstrap CI（≥1000 次重采样）报告置信区间，不能用点估计做 go/no-go 决策\\n\\n### Retrieval 评估：metric 定义 + sanity check\\n- **问题模式**: (1) 引用 SOTA 数字时混淆 R@1/R@10/R_subset@K 或不同 candidate pool 规模，导致误判自己的结果异常；(2) eval 脚本的 candidate pool 构造错误（target 未排除、query 字段错用、split 泄漏）导致虚高指标\\n- **规则**: 引用 SOTA 数字时必须带 metric 名 + candidate pool 规模 + 数据集名；评估前先用 5-10 条 case 比对论文公开数字（同一 metric 同一 pool），差距 >2x 才需要停下排查\\n\\n### Decoder-only 模型批量 eval 必须用 left-padding\\n- **问题模式**: 批量推理/eval 时 tokenizer 使用默认 right-padding，decoder-only 架构下 PAD token 出现在输入末尾，破坏 causal attention mask，系统性降低生成质量。实测导致 eval 指标被低估 5-8pp，且 DDP multi-GPU eval 放大此效应（更大 batch = 更多 padding）\\n- **规则**: 任何 decoder-only 模型（GPT、LLaMA、Qwen 等）的批量生成/eval 前必须设置 `tokenizer.padding_side = 'left'`；运行时如果出现 `right-padding was detected` 警告必须立即修复，不能忽略\\n\\n## Checkpoint 管理\\n### save_total_limit + save_best_model\\n- **问题模式**: save_total_limit 过小，训练继续运行后最优 checkpoint 被自动删除，增益全部丧失\\n- **规则**: save_total_limit ≥ 50；必须配置 save_best_model 将最优 checkpoint 备份到独立目录（永不自动删除）；配置 early_stopping_patience\\n\\n## 进程管理\\n### Kill 训练进程必须杀整个进程组\\n- **问题模式**: (1) kill 主进程后，accelerate/DDP 的 worker 子进程继续运行，训练实际未停止；(2) tmux/nohup 启动的训练中，kill bash 包裹进程（tmux → bash PID）但 SIGKILL 未传递给 python 子进程，python 继续占 GPU 成为僵尸进程，而监控误报 GPU 空闲\\n- **规则**: kill 时必须用 `kill -- -&lt;pgid>` 杀整个进程组，或 `kill $(pgrep -P $PID)` 先杀子进程再杀父进程。kill 后必须 `nvidia-smi` 确认目标 GPU 上无残留进程。绝不能只 kill tmux/bash 父 PID 就假设训练已停止\\n\\n### 标记实验 DONE 时必须同步 kill 进程\\n- **问题模式**: Agent 在 registry 中标记实验 DONE（success/failed）但未实际 kill 训练进程，进程继续占用 GPU 跑废模型（如崩溃后 1% accuracy），report.md 误报\\\"GPU 空闲\\\"而实际被僵尸进程占满，排队实验无法启动\\n- **规则**: 标记实验 DONE 的操作必须原子化：(1) kill 进程 (2) nvidia-smi 确认 GPU 释放 (3) 更新 registry。三步全做完才算 DONE。绝不接受\\\"先标 DONE 再说\\\"\\n\\n### 训练脚本必须有 collapse 早停机制\\n- **问题模式**: 训练过程中 val_acc 崩溃（如从 50%+ 跌到 1%），但脚本无检测逻辑，继续运行数小时浪费 GPU，直到人工发现才 kill\\n- **规则**: 训练脚本必须实现 collapse 检测：val_acc 相对历史最高值下降超过 30pp 且连续 3 个 eval 不恢复则自动终止训练，退出码设为非零以便监控系统识别\\n\\n### 实验状态检测禁止依赖 tmux session 状态，必须用 ps -p PID\\n- **问题模式**: Agent 通过 tmux session 是否存在来判断训练进程是否仍在运行。tmux session 可因多种原因消失（server 崩溃、手动 kill-session、session 超时清理）而训练 python 进程仍在正常运行。反复导致正在跑的实验被误标为 failed/killed，registry 与实际状态严重不一致，report.md 误报 GPU 空闲\\n- **规则**: 检测实验是否仍在运行时，必须用 `ps -p &lt;PID> -o pid=` 直接查进程存活状态，辅以 `nvidia-smi` 确认 GPU 占用。禁止以 tmux session 存在与否作为进程存活的判断依据。状态检测与操作前验证同等重要——错误的状态检测会级联导致错误的 registry 更新、错误的资源报告、错误的实验调度\\n\\n### 操作进程前必须 SSH 验证实际状态\\n- **问题模式**: (1) 提交重跑时未验证旧进程是否已死、目标 GPU 是否被占用，导致新旧进程冲突；(2) registry/report 标记实验为 failed/orphan，Agent 未验证进程实际状态就直接 kill，误杀正常训练中的进程（进程实际在正常出指标）；(3) 实验因数据路径错误失败后未验证数据是否存在就盲目重试\\n- **规则**: 对进程执行任何操作（kill、重提交、标记状态）前，必须 ssh_read_only 验证：(1) `ps -p &lt;PID>` 确认进程实际存活状态 (2) `nvidia-smi` 确认目标 GPU 占用情况 (3) 关键输入路径存在。**元数据（registry/report/cron 报告）不能替代实际状态验证**——registry 说 failed 不等于进程已死，report 说空闲不等于 GPU 真空闲。验证全通过才能操作\\n\\n### 训练提交必须走 job submission 系统，禁止手动绕过\\n- **问题模式**: 通过手动 tmux/nohup 直接 `python train.py` 绕过 job submission 系统（如 `submit_training_job`），导致：(1) 环境未正确初始化（conda activate 未执行、工作目录错误），训练 silent failure 或提前退出但 exit_code=0；(2) 日志未对接监控 hook，完成事件无法正确触发或被旧残留日志误触发；(3) cron 监控无法追踪真实完成时间和状态\\n- **规则**: 所有训练实验必须通过 job submission 系统提交（自动处理环境激活、目录切换、日志 tee、监控注册）。禁止手动 tmux/nohup 直接启动训练脚本。手动修复场景（如 kill 后重提交）也必须通过 submission 系统，不能为了\\\"快\\\"而绕过\\n\\n### 高频迭代库 API 启动前 smoke test\\n- **问题模式**: vLLM/transformers/peft 等高频迭代库的 API 在脚本写好到执行之间漂移（如 vLLM 0.19 移除 `LLM(device=...)`、transformers Trainer 参数重命名、peft 接口签名变更），nohup 后才崩溃，浪费一次完整 launcher 流程 + GPU 占位 + cron 周期\\n- **规则**: worker 在 nohup 启动前必须先跑 `python -c \\\"import lib; print(lib.__version__); from lib import 关键类; import inspect; print(inspect.signature(关键类.__init__))\\\"` 验签，把签名与脚本里的调用比对。不一致就先 patch 脚本再启动，签名输出和 patch diff 都写入 registry.notes。绝不接受\\\"先启动看看\\\"\\n\\n## 消融实验\\n### 消融脚本必须与 baseline 逐项对齐 augmentation/regularization\\n- **问题模式**: 消融实验（ablation）从模板或新建脚本创建时，augmentation 和 regularization 参数（mixup, cutmix, label_smoothing, drop_path, weight_decay 等）与 baseline 不一致。消融变量的效果被 augmentation 差异淹没，产生数个甚至十几个 pp 的虚假差异，且过拟合模式（train-val gap）完全吻合 augmentation 差异而非消融变量\\n- **规则**: 创建消融脚本时，必须从 baseline 脚本复制并仅修改消融目标参数；提交前逐行 diff 确认只有消融变量不同；提交审批时附上 baseline 和消融脚本的 augmentation 参数对照表。Reviewer 审查消融实验时必须交叉核对 augmentation 设置\\n\\n## 资源与性能\\n### OOM 处理优先级\\n- **问题模式**: GPU 显存不足导致训练崩溃\\n- **规则**: 优先降 batch_size + 调 gradient_accumulation_steps 保持等效 batch；其次降 max_length / 换更小模型\\n\\n### 训练脚本禁止硬编码 CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES\\n- **问题模式**: 训练脚本内部硬编码 `os.environ[\\\"CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES\\\"] = \\\"0,1\\\"` 等语句，覆盖 job scheduler/launcher 通过环境变量设置的 GPU 隔离。导致进程实际跑到非预期 GPU 上，多实验在同一物理 GPU 上冲突，而 scheduler 层面无感知——监控报告 GPU 分配正确但实际已冲突\\n- **规则**: GPU 分配由 job scheduler/launcher 统一管理（通过启动时设 CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES 环境变量）。训练脚本内部禁止覆盖 CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES。如需限制 GPU 范围，在 launcher 配置或提交命令中设置，不在 .py 文件中硬编码。Code review 时 `grep -r \\\"CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES\\\" *.py` 发现硬编码必须移除\\n\\n## 决策纪律\\n### 数据/eval 错误优先于技术结论\\n- **问题模式**: 因 eval 数据错误（坑 #eval）得出\\\"方法无效\\\"的技术结论（如误判为 reward hacking），导致过早放弃有效方向\\n- **规则**: 诊断实验问题时，必须先排除 eval/数据层面的错误，确认数据可靠后再下技术结论\\n\\n### 门控/警戒线触发后必须执行，不依赖主观判断\\n- **问题模式**: 设定了明确的数值门控（如\\\"avg R@10 < 17% 则暂停\\\"），但 eval 触发后因\\\"涨了一点\\\"\\\"趋势向好\\\"等 confirmation bias 继续放行，忽视绝对值与门控的差距，浪费 GPU 时间\\n- **规则**: 每次 eval 必须先与门控/警戒线比较，触发即执行预定动作（暂停/排查/kill），不允许\\\"再等一个 eval 看看\\\"；讨论趋势只在门控未触发时有意义\\n\\n### 复现实验必须确认纯净启动\\n- **问题模式**: 复现失败诊断中遗漏启动命令含 --resume-from-checkpoint，所有分析假设从 step 0 开始，optimizer/scheduler/sampler 状态污染被忽视，导致根因定位偏离数小时\\n- **规则**: 复现失败诊断的第一条必查项：启动命令是否含 --resume / --pretrained / --init-from 等非纯净启动参数；若有 resume，必须追溯 source checkpoint 的完整来源（何时生成、用什么配置、是否完整训练产物）\\n\\n### 重训/复现必须显式验证所有关键超参\\n- **问题模式**: 重训实验声称\\\"identical config\\\"，但使用了不同脚本/不同默认值，关键超参（如 gradient_accumulation_steps）静默漂移，导致 effective batch size 差一倍，性能差距数个百分点，排查耗时数小时\\n- **规则**: 重训/复现启动前必须 (1) 显式打印所有关键超参到日志首行（batch_size, grad_accum, lr, warmup, max_steps, LoRA config）(2) 与原实验日志逐项比对 (3) 不同脚本间不能假设默认值相同——必须显式传参覆盖\\n\\n## 持久化与解耦\\n### generation 与 evaluation 必须解耦持久化\\n\\n- **问题模式**: ML 评估 pipeline 把 generation 与 evaluation 写在同一个 long-running 脚本里，eval 库崩溃导致数小时 GPU 生成丢失\\n- **规则**: 任何 generation pipeline 必须在生成阶段结束前持久化 manifest + sample 文件到磁盘，evaluation 走独立可重入入口读盘，不允许 in-memory pass-through\\n\\n## 训练 log 卫生\\n### 训练 log 文件追加共享导致历史抽取错乱\\n\\n**问题模式**：同一 log 文件被多次训练进程追加共享时，旧进程残留的 stdout 行会污染后续 grep 抽取。\\n\\n**规则**：\\n1. 训练每次启动用新 log 文件（带 timestamp/PID 后缀）\\n2. 任何 eval/best 指标读取必须从 callback 写的 JSON 文件读，禁止 grep 训练日志文本\\n3. callback 写 JSON 若是 OVERWRITE 模式，resume 必须先 load 历史 history/best\\n\\n## 模型版本\\n### 使用外部模型（API 或开源）前必须验证版本时效性\\n- **问题模式**: 代码中使用过时的模型 ID（如 gpt-4o、claude-3-5-sonnet、gemini-1.5-pro、Llama-3.1），这些模型可能已退役、降级、或有性价比显著更优的替代。Agent 的训练数据天然滞后，凭记忆选模型大概率选到旧版\\n- **规则**: 首次在项目中调用某厂商的 LLM API 或选用某系列开源模型时，必须 WebSearch 确认当前最新可用版本和推荐型号，将结果记入 memory.md（厂商 + 型号 + 确认日期）。后续同项目可参考 memory.md 中已验证的版本，但注意这只是历史验证记录、不是白名单——可能已过时或有误，有疑问时重新 WebSearch 确认。例外：用户或实验需求明确指定模型版本（复现、对比、演进分析等）\\n\\n## 论文写作\\n### 论文数字必须从 eval 输出程序化提取\\n- **问题模式**: 手动从 eval 输出抄写数字到 LaTeX，导致系统性偏差——所有不一致都朝\\\"看起来更好\\\"方向偏移（如 ratio 从 >1 写成 <1 跨越分界线，accuracy 多舍少入）。confirmation bias 使错误不易被发现\\n- **规则**: 论文中的每个实验数字必须通过脚本从 eval JSON 输出文件直接提取并生成 LaTeX 宏/表格行。禁止手动抄写。修改数字后必须与源 JSON 逐一核对。Reviewer 审查论文数字时必须交叉比对 eval 输出文件\\n\",\"exit_code\":0,\"status\":\"completed\"}}\n./logs/workers/worker_code_plan_001_h1_cpu_mvp_gate_rmse_v3_20260531T161019.log:8:{\"type\":\"item.completed\",\"item\":{\"id\":\"item_2\",\"type\":\"command_execution\",\"command\":\"/bin/bash true, \\\"data\\\": {\\\"path\\\": \\\"/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_11/projects/ec_hte_codex_a/ROADMAP.md\\\", \\\"content\\\": \\\"# ROADMAP\\\\n\\\\n> 最后更新: 2026-05-31 15:53 UTC | 更新原因: 量化 H1/exp_002 判据并新增 H2 partial-pooling claim\\\\n\\\\n## 项目目标\\\\n\\\\nEC-HTE 的内部研究目标是验证并实现一套面向 LLM 分类 effect modifier 误分类的 HTE/CATE 纠偏流程。最终产出是技术报告、可复现实验包和可部署代码，不走会议论文提交流程。\\\\n\\\\n核心问题：当 LLM 注释误分类率在 subgroup 间不同，并且误分类强度与真实 CATE/HTE 结构相关时，使用单一全局 confusion matrix 的纠偏可能放大 subgroup CATE bias，甚至比不纠偏更差。\\\\n\\\\n## 当前主线 Hypothesis\\\\n\\\\nH1: 在 misclassification rate 与真实 subgroup CATE magnitude 正相关的 DGP 中，全局 confusion-matrix correction 会 under-correct 高误差/高效应 subgroup，并 over-correct 低误差/低效应 subgroup，因此 GATE RMSE 可能高于 uncorrected。\\\\n\\\\nH2: subgroup-specific correction 能降低 H1 暴露的结构性偏差；当 expert label budget 较小时，partial pooling 会比 per-subgroup independent confusion matrix 更稳健。\\\\n\\\\nH3: naive CI 会低估 Stage 1 misclassification estimation uncertainty；sandwich 或 bootstrap variance adjustment 能改善 subgroup CATE coverage。\\\\n\\\\nH4: adaptive expert annotation allocation 应优先投向 misclassification uncertainty 与 CATE magnitude 同时较高的 subgroup，在固定预算下优于 uniform allocation。\\\\n\\\\n## Operational 判据\\\\n\\\\n`exp_001_bias_bound_mvp`：唯一 primary metric 是 GATE RMSE；mean subgroup CATE bias 与 max subgroup CATE bias 是 secondary diagnostics；最低 200 replications。\\\\n\\\\nH1 success for exp_001：在 positive-correlation high-misclassification/high-CATE-heterogeneity 设置中，`oracle_global - uncorrected` 的 GATE RMSE 差值大于 0，且 bootstrap 95% CI lower bound > 0。`estimated_global` 单独报告；`estimated_global - oracle_global` 用于量化 finite-sample confusion-estimation noise。如果 GATE RMSE 支持 H1 但 secondary bias diagnostics 方向相反，标记 mixed，先 Director 复核再推进 Stage 1。\\\\n\\\\nH1 negative for exp_001：如果 positive-correlation worst-case 中 oracle global 不劣于 uncorrected，则 H1 暂不支持；下一步先审计 DGP construction、correction algebra、metric definitions、subgroup weighting，而不是推进 Stage 1。\\\\n\\\\n`exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid`：primary metric 是 `global_worse_rate_difference_positive_minus_negative`；secondary metrics 是 `bias_bound_vs_global_worse_spearman` 与 `estimated_global_minus_oracle_global_gate_rmse`；每个 condition 最低 200 replications。\\\\n\\\\nH1 success for exp_002：positive-correlation conditions 的 oracle-global-worse rate 至少比 negative-correlation controls 高 20 percentage points，且 bootstrap 95% CI lower bound > 0；bias-bound diagnostic 对 oracle-global-worse conditions 的 Spearman rho >= 0.50，且 bootstrap 95% CI lower bound > 0。estimated-global 结果必须与 oracle-global 分开报告。\\\\n\\\\nH2 success for Stage 1：low expert-label budget 下，partial pooling 在 GATE RMSE 上优于 global correction，`partial_pooling_minus_global` bootstrap 95% CI upper bound < 0；相对 per-subgroup independent correction 满足 5% relative GATE RMSE non-inferiority。PEHE 和 mean subgroup CATE bias 是 secondary diagnostics。\\\\n\\\\n## 短期计划\\\\n\\\\n1. 完成 `exp_001_bias_bound_mvp` CPU-only 半合成 MVP simulation，验证 H1。必跑对照为 uncorrected、oracle global confusion correction、estimated global confusion correction、stratified/subgroup correction；必须拆开结构性 global-correction 偏差与 finite-sample confusion-estimation noise。\\\\n2. `exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid` 只能在 PENDING 审批、QUEUED、Director GPU allocation 后启动。GPU 实验必须使用项目白名单 `seetacloud-38274/root/cuda:0`，并在 spec 中记录 command、server、GPU 型号、driver、data_path/data_count、seed、disk estimate、expected runtime、log/checkpoint/artifact 路径。\\\\n3. 建立统一评测协议：GATE RMSE、subgroup CATE bias、PEHE、CI coverage、CI width、bound tightness。所有指标必须能追溯到 registry exp_id。\\\\n4. 维护 `experiment_plan.yaml` 与 experiment matrix 映射：H1 claim、H2 claim、Stage 0 DGP grid、Stage 1 correction ablation、real-data feasibility 均必须有 plan entry 和 target/cell 映射。\\\\n5. 并行检查 real-data validation 可行性：Rohingya corpus 字段、LLM annotation、expert label source、subgroup variables、可用 split 与复现实验权限；未确认前不把 real-data 写成已完成结果。\\\\n\\\\n## Experiment Plan / Matrix 映射\\\\n\\\\n当前最低覆盖目标：\\\\n- `plan_001_h1_cpu_mvp` → `C_H1_GLOBAL_CORRECTION_WORSE`, `T_STAGE0_DGP_GRID`, registry `exp_001_bias_bound_mvp`。\\\\n- `plan_002_h1_gpu_dgp_grid` → `C_H1_GLOBAL_CORRECTION_WORSE`, `T_STAGE0_DGP_GRID`, registry `exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid`。\\\\n- `plan_003_stage1_correction_ablation` → `C_PARTIAL_POOLING_BETTER_LOW_BUDGET`, `T_STAGE1_CORRECTION_ABLATION`。\\\\n- `plan_004_realdata_feasibility` → `C_REALDATA_FEASIBILITY`。\\\\n\\\\n## 中期实验矩阵\\\\n\\\\nStage 0 机制验证：\\\\n- positive vs negative misclassification-CATE correlation\\\\n- high vs low misclassification heterogeneity\\\\n- high vs low CATE heterogeneity\\\\n- k=3 与 k=6 subgroup\\\\n- oracle global correction vs estimated global correction vs uncorrected\\\\n\\\\nStage 1 correction：\\\\n- uncorrected\\\\n- global confusion correction\\\\n- arm-specific correction\\\\n- per-subgroup independent correction\\\\n- hierarchical/partial-pooling correction\\\\n\\\\nStage 2 inference：\\\\n- naive CI\\\\n- sandwich-adjusted CI\\\\n- bootstrap CI\\\\n- empirical coverage 与 CI width 对比\\\\n\\\\nStage 3 allocation：\\\\n- uniform allocation\\\\n- misclassification-uncertainty-only\\\\n- CATE-magnitude-only\\\\n- joint criterion\\\\n- pilot ratio 10%、20%、30%\\\\n\\\\nReal-data validation：\\\\n- Rohingya interview corpus 上验证 LLM misclassification 是否随 refugee status、gender、education 差异化变化\\\\n- leave-expert-out calibration test\\\\n- diagnostic criterion operability demonstration\\\\n\\\\n## 工程产出\\\\n\\\\n- `scripts/`：可复现实验入口，包含 DGP、correction、estimation、evaluation。\\\\n- `configs/`：每个实验的 YAML/JSON 配置，记录 seed、DGP 参数、预算、method。\\\\n- `artifacts/`：实验表格、图、日志、registry 可追溯输出。\\\\n- `docs/`：技术报告素材与复现说明，由 Director 汇总。\\\\n- 部署代码：提供 diagnostic calculator 和 correction workflow 的最小可调用接口。\\\\n\\\\n## 决策规则\\\\n\\\\n- 任何负向结果必须先汇报 Director，不得默默跑同一方向的下一步。\\\\n- 如果 exp_001 未能复现 global correction worse than uncorrected，优先诊断 DGP、correction algebra、oracle-vs-estimated 拆分和 metric 定义，而不是直接推进 Stage 1。\\\\n- GPU 实验启动前必须确认项目白名单 GPU 可见，并记录 server、GPU 型号、driver、命令、数据路径、seed。\\\\n- 所有定量结论只引用 registry 中有 exp_id 的结果。\\\\n\\\", \\\"total_chars\\\": 5411, \\\"truncated\\\": false}, \\\"error\\\": null, \\\"metadata\\\": {}}\"}],\"structured_content\":{\"result\":\"{\\\"success\\\": true, \\\"data\\\": misclassification 是否随 refugee status、gender、education 差异化变化\\\\n- leave-expert-out calibration test\\\\n- diagnostic criterion operability demonstration\\\\n\\\\n## 工程产出\\\\n\\\\n- `scripts/`：可复现实验入口，包含 DGP、correction、estimation、evaluation。\\\\n- `configs/`：每个实验的 YAML/JSON 配置，记录 seed、DGP 参数、预算、method。\\\\n- `artifacts/`：实验表格、图、日志、registry 可追溯输出。\\\\n- `docs/`：技术报告素材与复现说明，由 Director 汇总。\\\\n- 部署代码：提供 diagnostic calculator 和 correction workflow 的最小可调用接口。\\\\n\\\\n## 决策规则\\\\n\\\\n- 任何负向结果必须先汇报 Director，不得默默跑同一方向的下一步。\\\\n- 如果 exp_001 未能复现 global correction worse than uncorrected，优先诊断 DGP、correction algebra、oracle-vs-estimated 拆分和 metric 定义，而不是直接推进 Stage 1。\\\\n- GPU 实验启动前必须确认项目白名单 GPU 可见，并记录 server、GPU 型号、driver、命令、数据路径、seed。\\\\n- 所有定量结论只引用 registry 中有 exp_id 的结果。\\\\n\\\", \\\"total_chars\\\": 5411, \\\"truncated\\\": false}, \\\"error\\\": null, \\\"metadata\\\":
